Inyigisho
 
 Kanama 2026 
 
 
 
Inyandiko ziri hasi aha ni inyandiko z'ibitekerezo n'ibyavumbuwe byagiye biza mu mutwe wanjye uko igihe gihita. Akenshi ntibiba byararangijwe cyangwa ngo bishyirwe mu bikorwa, nubwo mu gihe kizaza bishobora kuzaba. Cyangwa ntibishobore, kuko kuri bimwe mu bitekerezo, inyandiko y'inyibutso irahagije. Bimwe ni inzira z'ibihuhwe bityo ntibikenewe gukomeza kubiteza imbere. Ariko nubwo bimeze bityo, bishobora kuba bikwiye kwandikwa.
 
 2023-02-24:  IBIKOSA BY'AMAYUNGURURO Y'IMYITWARIRE Y'UBWONKO
 
Namenye ku bw'amahirwe igihe nakoraga ko iyo ngerageje gukura ifoto muri Photoshop nkoresheje Neural Filters kandi iyo foto ikaba iri mu bwoko bwa CMYK ikaba irimo ibice byinshi byeruye, habaho ikosa ridasanzwe ariko akenshi riryoheye ijisho.

 2023-10-04:  INZIRA Z'IBY'AKAMERE
 
Nahinduye imyirondoro y'iby'akamere (genetic sequences) mu bishushanyo nkoresheje itegeko ryoroshye: A bivuze intambwe ijya mu majyaruguru, T/U ijya mu majyepfo, C ijya mu burasirazuba na G ijya mu burengerazuba. Uruhererekane rw'ibishingirwaho bityo rugena ishusho y'umurongo ukomeje.
 
Ibishushanyo byavuyemo: koncepty-2023-10-04-geneticke-trajektorie.webp [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2023-10-04-geneticke-trajektorie.webp]

 2024-03-22:  UMUVUDUKO W'IBY'AMASO MU GUKORA KURI KOMPYUTA
 
Nakoze porogaramu ifata buri gukanda kuri klavye no gukanda kuri souris mu gihe nkora kuri kompyuta. Igihe kigenda mu cyerekezo cyo kwandika bisanzwe, kuva ibumoso ujya iburyo no kuva hejuru ujya hasi. Akabara k'umukara kavuze kwinjiza amakuru, ubuso bwera bukavuga ko nta kintu cyinjijwe.

 2024-08-19:  IBIHANGANO BYA KUSHÍK
 
Nakoreraga kuri kompyuta, ariko imbeba yanjye Kushík yakomeje kunyivangira. Yazamukaga ku mubiri wanjye, ishaka kwitabwaho, izamuka ku meza, kuri kompyuta. Yatangiye kuncanga gato. Nuko ndarakara, mfungurira inyandiko ya MS Word irimo ubusa maze mbwira imbeba ikintu nk'uko niba ishaka gukora ibyo nkora, nireke ikore.
 Nuko Kushík atangira kwiruka kuri klavye.
 
Iyi nyandiko niyo yavuyemo: koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf]
 
 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Nakoze porogaramu yanjye bwite yo kureba no guhindura amafoto y'ubushyuhe (thermographic) kuri kamera yanjye ya Seek Thermal RevealPRO. Ntabwo nabashije kubona porogaramu ikwiye kuri Windows kuri iyi kamera, ibyo bikaba ari ikibazo kuko dosiye zayo z'ubushyuhe zibika amakuru y'ubushyuhe ashobora gukoreshwa.
 
Porogaramu nayise Thermozepam, kuko nishimiye igishushanyo cyayo kandi kuyikoresha bimbere nk'ikiyobyabwenge. Porogaramu ifungura dosiye za TIFF z'ubushyuhe, igafasha guhindura ikarita y'amabara y'ifoto ikoresheje colormap yo mu isomero rya matplotlib, gupima ubushyuhe buringaniye bw'ifoto, kumenya ubushyuhe bwinshi, kugaragaza ubushyuhe buke n'bwinshi, histogramu, ndetse no kugaragaza ubushyuhe bw'akabara kamwe iyo ukandeho. Iyo ubika, ishobora kohereza ifoto ya PNG yahinduriwe amabara kandi ikayongerera ubunini ikoresheje interpolasiyo.
 
Ifoto ya porogaramu: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png]
 Inyandiko y'isoko: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  UMURABYO MU MWIJIMA
 
Natekereje gukora umushinga wa Arduino, aho nashyira laser mu gasanduku (kabanje gusukurwa n'itara rya UV-C). Iyo laser yajya irekura umurabyo w'isegonda rimwe mu gihe kitazwi (kugira ngo ntamenya igihe nyacyo byabereye, ariko nzi ko bishobora kubaho mu minota 10 ku kigero cya 100%). Niba nabishobora neza, uwo murabyo w'urumuri ntiwamenywa n'uwari we wese.
 
 2026-01-31:  IYIGISHO RY'IFOTO YA PINHOLE
 
Nagerageje gukora ifoto ya pinhole ikora neza muri Python. Kera mu mashuri yisumbuye nari narakoze ifoto nyayo, ariko numvaga ko iyo nyigize iyigisho, nzasobanukirwa neza imikorere yayo, kuko bidahagije gusa gucukura akenge mu gasanduku, gushyiramo urupapuro rwakira urumuri, hanyuma rugafotora, n'ibindi. Hano, ahubwo, n'ubumenyi bw'ibintu bigomba kwigishwa.
 Nagize umuhate wo gukora iyigisho ry'imibare rirushijeho kuba iry'ukuri, nubwo imbogamizi ari ubumenyi bwanjye n'imbaraga za mudasobwa zihari. Porogaramu, urugero, ikoresha forward ray tracing, ni ukuvuga gukurikirana urumuri ruva ku isoko rugana ku ifoto, aho gukoresha backward ray tracing isanzwe, ikurikira imirasire iva ku ifoto igana ku isoko. Uburyo bwa forward bwegereye icyerekezo cy'urumuri nyacyo, ariko akenshi ntibuba bufite imbaraga mu kubara.
 
Ingero z'imigirire ya porogaramu n'ibisobanuro by'ibipimo: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Inyandiko y'isoko: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  GUTONDA IBIKARA BY'AMAFOTO
 
Nagerageje gukoresha porogaramu kugira ngo ntonde ibikara by'amafoto. Igitekerezo cyari ukutonda ibikara ukurikije HUE n'ubucucike: ku ruhande rumwe (nka x) gutonda ibikara ukurikije HUE, ku rundi ruhande (nka y) ukurikije ubucucike. Ibi bituma ibikara by'ifoto byongera gutondekwa, ariko nta na kimwe kigenda cyangwa ngo havuke gishya. Nubwo bimeze bityo, amafoto yavuyemo atakaza ubusobanuro bwayo bwa mbere, ariko agumana ikirere cy'amabara.
 
Inyandiko y'isoko: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  KUGENDA KU INTERNET KU BW'AMAHIRWE
 
Namaranye ibyumweru bibiri ngenzura internet ku bw'amahirwe, kuko nifuzaga kumenya uko imera iyo itayobowe n'imashini z'ubwenge.
 
Uburyo bwa mbere (bworoshye) bwari ugukora porogaramu yinjiza kandi ifungura URL zikozwe ku bw'amahirwe. Ibi byari ibintu bidafite akamaro, kuko umwanya wa URL zishoboka ari munini cyane kandi izihari zikaba ari nke.
 
Uburyo bwa kabiri bwari ugukora crawler yanjye bwite nkayiha URL y'intangiriro idasanzwe (nka urubuga kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). Ibyavuyemo nagerageje kubiyungurura nkoresheje LLM hanyuma nkareba gusa ibyo bishimishije (ukurikije ibipimo bimwe na bimwe). Nabonye ibintu byinshi bidasanzwe byo kwishimisha, ariko nta kintu gihambaye.
 
Uburyo bwa gatatu bwari bwiza kandi bwishimishije. Navumbuye ko hari Certstream. Ni serivisi nziza itambutsa imyirondoro y'umutekano yashyizweho ku isi hose. Bityo, igaragaza URL z'imbuga zishya cyangwa izivugurura imyirondoro yazo.
 
Inyandiko y'isoko: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Namaranye iminsi myinshi nkanza kuri izo URL. Akenshi nahuraga n'uburiganya (cryptocurrency, n'ibindi), porunogarafiya, AI, AI porunogarafiya, amabwiriza y'uko wakora AI porunogarafiya, urusimbi, imbuga zo kwinjira mu masisteme, n'imbuga zikiri kuvuka (zifite ibirango cyangwa amagambo "turacyabikora").
 
Kubera ubu buryo, nahuye n'urubuga treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], rwagumye mu mutwe wanjye kubera imiterere yarwo yoroshye n'uburyo rudashishikajwe no gukurura abantu. Nanamenye ko urwo rubuga ari agace gato ka internet, kuko kugeza ubu Google itararushyira mu byo yerekana. Internet Archive yarubonye, ariko binyuze mu makuru ya CT, kimwe nanjye. Gushaka byarangiye bigize akamaro.
 
 2026-05-06:  IYEREKANA MU PACIFIC
 
Natekereje gukora amafoto 360° ya Pacific akozwe na AI. Hanyuma nkayashyiramo QR code nkoresheje AI (nk'uburyo bwa Illusion Diffusion), ijyana ku buhanzi runaka. Hanyuma, byashoboka gushyiramo amakuru y'aho ifoto yafatiwe (nka GPS hagati muri Pacific) no kuyipakira kuri Google Maps.
 
Uburyo ubundi bwari ugufata ahantu nyaho hafashwe n'ifoto ya 360°, nko mu mujyi umwe, hanyuma nkahindura imyirondoro ya GPS yaho nkayijyana muri Pacific. Impamvu mvuga gusa amafoto ya 360° ni uko Google kugeza ubu yemera kuyapakira ku myirondoro ya GPS nyayo ikoresheje metadata. Mu gihe amafoto asanzwe agomba gushyirwa ahantu hazwi.
 
 2026-06-04:  IBIBYIMBYE BY'AMABARA
 
Uyu mushinga nagerageje kuwushyira mu bikorwa, ariko kubera igihe kinini wari gutwara, sinawurangije. Nakoze inyandiko y'amaboko gusa, nkuraho ibibara (transparent background), ngumana ubunini bwabyo nuko nkora porogaramu yo kubitonda mu murongo. Icyakora, iyi ni inyandiko yagombaga gusobanura igitekerezo:
 
«Mu mwaka wa 2026 nanditse buri kintu cyose kigaragara ku mubiri wanjye. Buri kintu cyagumanye ubunini bwacyo. Hanyuma, ibyo bibyimba byose byaciwe mu buryo bw'ikoranabuhanga bitondwa mu murongo.»
 
 2026-06-21:  POROGARAMU Y'UBWENGE YIHINDUZA
 
Nagerageje gukora porogaramu ikoresha OpenAI API n'imashini zayo za LLM. Porogaramu ifite inshingano yo kwihinduza ubwayo hanyuma ikongera kwitangiza. Kandi nifuzaga kumenya aho byazageza.
 Ikibabaje, imashini akenshi yanze gukora ibyo nayisabye, kuko yabonaga ko ari ibintu biteje akaga. Cyangwa yarabikoze, ariko iyayikurikiye ntiyabikora. Icyo yageragezaga gukora cyari ukwanga kubera impamvu z'umutekano. Nta n'ubwo yigeze igera ku ntera ya kane.
 
Inyandiko y'isoko: koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py]
 
 2026-07-30:  SISITEMU Y'IBICE BIDAHOZE
 
Sisitemu yacu y'ubumenyi bw'ibintu irashimishije mu buryo bwo kuba bisa n'aho uduce duto cyane ari tumwe. Elektroni nk'elektroni, fotoni nk'ifotoni.
 
Ariko njye nifuza gukora iyigisho rya sisitemu ifite uduce twinshi, ariko nta na tubiri dusa. Hanyuma, nageze ku buryo aho uduce ubwabyo nta biranga cyangwa imyirondoro bifite. Bigenwa gusa n'uko bwirirwa, ibyo bikaba ari kimwe n'uko umuntu abibona ari muri sisitemu.
 
Ingaruka z'iki gitekerezo zindyohera kuzitekereza. Iyo mbifashe mu buryo bwo gukabya, kubaho muri sisitemu nk'iyo byaba bigoye gukora ibyo kumenya ibizaza. Nanone, ibintu bibaho muri sisitemu nk'iyo bishobora kuba byagombye kwitondera gutakaza cyangwa kwibwa kw'uduce twabyo.
 
Nagerageje kuyigisha kandi mu buryo bwa tekiniki nakoze inshingano. Icyakora, ntabwo byari ibintu bishimishije cyane. Ntekereza ko ari uko kugira ngo ubashe kubona ibintu bishimishije, ukeneye imbaraga nyinshi za mudasobwa (uduce twinshi, igihe kinini mu iyigisho, wenda n'ibindi bice by'iyigisho) n'isesengura ryimbitse ry'ibivuyemo.
 
Ivugurura rya 2026-08-22: Mu gihe narebaga ibintu byavuye mu iyigisho, nageze ku bintu bitangaje byiyubaka. Ni ibintu bimeze nk'imizingo y'amagi, bifite ishusho y'uruziga cyangwa y'igi. Bifite inyuma n'imbere bigaragara neza, aho urukuta cyangwa «membrana» y'inyuma ari ikintu gihamye, mu gihe imbere ari ho hantu hihuta cyane. Iyi ntangiriro nayishyize nk'agaciro k'ibanze muri demo iri hasi.
 
Icyitonderwa: iterambere rya sisitemu, bityo n'ubunini bw'ibyo bintu, biterwa n'ubwiza bw'ishusho. Umuvuduko w'amashusho ugira ingaruka ku guhama kwabyo, wenda kubera amakosa y'imibare mu gihe cy'intambwe ndende. Nubwo ibyo bintu byasenuka, nko nyuma yo kongera umuvuduko w'amashusho, sisitemu yongera kwiyubaka ikarema ibindi bishya.
 
Ibi si ibintu nateganyaga gukora. Nakoze gusa sisitemu ifite částicemi/interakcemi idasanzwe nuko ibi biba nk'ikintu cyivumbuye. Biratangaje, ubu mfite iyigisho ry'«ibintu by'amavuta».
 
Idemo ry'imikoranire: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  IMYANDA
 
Byagenda gute nifotoye buri mwanda wose njugunya mu kigega? Byashoboka kuwufotora n'ikigereranyo, hanyuma imyanda igakurwaho inyuma, ikajyanwa mu mbonerahamwe ihuje n'ubunini bwayo.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
Ubwo nagishaga inama ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) ku buryo bworoshye bwo gusobanura igerageza RY'IMYITWARIRE Y'AKAZI KU MASHINI (reba hejuru), yahise yita iyo chat "Ibisobanuro bya porogaramu  天天中彩票买 ". Icyakora, iyo chat yari nshya rwose, yaremewe gusobanura iryo gerageza gusa. Ibirimo bya porogaramu nayihaye byari bisanzwe rwose.
 
Nuko rero mfata umwanzuro wo kuyibaza ibyo, nandika ibi:
 
"Kuki wwise iyi chat Ibisobanuro bya porogaramu  天天中彩票买 ?"
 
Igisubizo cyayo cyari:
 
"Sinzi. 😭 Iyo nyuguti "զ" ni inyuguti y'ikinyarumeniya "za", bityo "Ibisobanuro bya porogaramu զ" ntibyumvikana na gato mu rurimi rw'igicehe. Amazina ya chat agenda yikora yikora biturutse ku ntangiriro y'ikiganiro, kandi hano umushini ukora amazina wagaragaye nk'uwahisemo inyuguti y'ikinyarumeniya ku bw'impanuka. Mu birimo ikiganiro nta mpamvu mbonamo. Bityo rero, si ikimenyetso cy'ibanga cya porogaramu. Ni "Ibisobanuro bya porogaramu" gusa hamwe n'inyuguti y'amahanga."
 
Nyuma y'ibindi biganiro bike, yaje kumenya ko atari ikinyarumeniya, ahubwo ari igishinwa. Icyakora, biragaragara ko yabanje kwijijisha cyane. Ntekereza ko ijambo " 天天中彩票买 " rikora nk'ikimenyetso kizwi nabi cya " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Simvuze ko rikora kimwe, cyangwa ngo menye niba tokenizer ifata ijambo " 天天中彩票买 " nk'ikimenyetso kimwe. Icyakora, iyo moderi iyo irikoresha yitwara nk'aho ari ChatGPT 3 ifite ikimenyetso cya " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Kandi ntibishoboka ko bitajyanye n'ubwenge bwayo, kuko bugera no hanze y'iyi chat.
 
Iyi chat yose yoherejwe nka PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  AKABUTO K'INZOZI ZANJYE
 
Mpora narakazwa n'amadirishya ya Windows atanga ibintu nko kubika amakuru kuri OneDrive, kuvugurura n'ibindi. Buri gihe batanga amahitamo hagati ya "Tangira" na "Ibuka nyuma". Ibyo birababaza cyane iyo ntashaka na kimwe muri ibyo.
 
Nuko ntekereza gukora ishusho (fotomontage), aho nshyira amahitamo ya gatatu - akabuto k'inzozi zanjye, katabaho muri sisitemu kugeza ubu. Iryo kaba ari akabuto "Genda uziyicire". Nizeye ko atari njye wenyine uwo shusho yakwibwira.
 
 2026-09-18:  KALKULATERI Y'UBUBWENGE BUHANITSE
 
Natekereje ubundi buryo budafite akamaro, nshaka kugerageza mu bitekerezo.
 
Ni porogaramu izakora nka kalkulateri yoroheje cyane. Ishobora kuba ishobora guteranya imibare yuzuye gusa, nta kindi. Icyakora, itandukaniro ni uko igisubizo kitazaboneka hano, ahubwo kizoherezwa kuri moderi y'ubwenge buhanitse (urugero: ChatGPT-6 Astra). Iyo moderi ikazaba ifite inshingano yo kubara icyo kibazo cyoroheje no kugarura igisubizo gusa.
 
Mbona ari ibintu biteye urwenya, gukoresha moderi y'ubwenge buhanitse ku kintu cyoroheje cyane. Mu by'ukuri, ni nko gukora parodi y'uko ibintu byoroheje ubu bishyirwa mu maboko ya AI, n'uko ubucuruzi bupfukiranya byose n'ikirango "AI" nk'aho ari uburozi bw'imikorere.
 
Mu by'ukuri, izaba mbi cyane kuruta kalkulateri isanzwe, itizewe, ifite gutinda, ihenze, itangiza ibidukikije, isaba kwihuza na interineti, ...

============================================================
Imyambaro
 
 Kanama 2026 
 
 
 
Amashusho ari hasi aha ni masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] yakozwe muri Adobe Photoshop mu gihe nakoraga imishinga itandukanye. Buri mwambaro wagiye ubikwa nk'ishusho yihariye mu gihe cyo kuwurema. Iyi nteranyirizo y'amashusho si igisubizo cy'itoranywa. Iyo umwambaro uremwe mu gihe cy'akazi, uba ari igice cyayo. Amashusho arondetse mu buryo bw'igihe kuva ku ya kera kugeza ku ya vuba aha.

TITLE: Abashakira indonke
SLUG: prizivnici
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/prizivnici/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-05-21
SOURCE: src/content/posts/prizivnici.mdx
BUILT_HTML: prizivnici/index.html
============================================================
Abashakira indonke
 
 Gicuransi 2026 
 
 
 
Mu mwaka ushize, incuro nyinshi ku munsi nagiye nshira ibisigazwa by'icyayi cy'amababi hamwe n'amazi asigaye ahantu hamwe mu rwuri i Plzeň-Božkov, kuko ntashaka ko imiyoboro y'amazi iziba.
 
Aho hantu ni ho ibinyamanswa byatangiye kwibumbira kuko birya amababi y'icyayi.
 
Rimwe na rimwe usangaho nka cumi na bibiri, ubundi ntubone na kimwe.
 
Duhuje ibyo dukunda.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg | ibinyamanswa n'icyayi]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg]

TITLE: Inyandiko ya Metaweb
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Inyandiko ya Metaweb
 
 Mata 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFESTO
 
Uru rubuga ni umushinga utimukanwa ugamije kubungabunga ibirimo mu gihe kirekire.
 
Ibirimo byose bibitswe mu madosiye, nta kwishingikiriza kuri base de données cyangwa sisitemu zo hanze. Uru rubuga rugaragara rukorwa nk'umusaruro utimukanwa, ushobora gukopororwa byoroshye, kubikwa no kongera gukorwa.
 
Umushinga wakwirakwijwe muri sisitemu nyinshi zigenga: ububiko, serivisi z'ububiko n'amafoto y'imashini (snapshots) atimukanwa. Ibi bigabanya ibyago byo gutakaza amakuru.
 
Intego ni ukwemeza ko bishoboka kongera kubaka ibirimo niyo ibice bimwe by'imitwe remezo y'umwimerere byaba bitakiriho.
 
URWEGO RW'UBUBIKO BWO KURI INTERINETI
 
Iyi mirongo ihagarariye umushinga umwe ubitswe muri sisitemu zitandukanye no mu buryo butandukanye.
 
Irimo ububiko bw'isoko nyamukuru, kopi zayo n'amafoto y'imashini (snapshots) yuzuye. Hamwe, akora imiterere y'inyongera yateguriwe kubungabunga umushinga no kwemerera kongera kuwubaka niyo ibice bimwe byaba bitakiboneka.
 
Umushinga ubaho kandi nk'ipaki ya Python CLI ikwirakwizwa binyuze kuri GitHub na PyPI, harimo n'amafoto y'imashini (fallback snapshots) y'inyandiko zatoranijwe.
 
Kubika mu buryo bw'ikoranabuhanga uru rubuga bityo bikoresha uretse izina ry'urubuga ryayo bwite, serivisi za GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku na paste.rs.
 
[CODE BLOCK]

Reba ikarita yuzuye y'ububiko (ARCHIVE.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ARCHIVE.txt]
 
INYANDIKO Z'IKORANABUHANGA
 
Inyandiko z'ikoranabuhanga zikenewe kugira ngo uru rubuga rwongere kubakwa.
 
Reba inyandiko zuzuye z'ikoranabuhanga [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
IPAKI Y'ISOKO YO KONGERA KUBAKA
 
Ipaki y'isoko y'urubuga ikorwa mu buryo bwikora igihe cyo gukora (build).
 
Imbere mu paki harimo kode y'isoko y'uru rubuga, manifest y'imitungo, imibare y'igenzura SHA-256 na script ya Python idafite ibyo ishingikirizaho byo hanze. Nta mitungo minini irimo ivuye kuri public/images/, public/files/ na public/demos/, kugira ngo ingano y'urubuga ruriho itiyongera ubusa. Nyuma yo kuyifungura, script ibanza kugerageza gukoporora imitungo mu buryo bw'aho hantu kuva ku gikorwa cyarangiye (build) hanyuma ikayikura ku mirongo rusange.
 
Bityo rero, kode y'isoko ikora build, naho build ikora kode y'isoko.
 
Ipaki y'isoko irashobora kongera kubakwa mu buryo bwikurikiranya: nyuma yo gukuramo imitungo no gushyiraho ibyo ishingikirizaho, bishoboka kuyikoresha kugira ngo urubuga rwongere gukorwa ndetse n'ipaki nshya y'isoko.
 
Kuramo ipaki y'isoko y'urubuga ishobora kongera kubakwa (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
UBWIRINZI N'UMWIRONDORO WA BUILD
 
SHA-256 igenzura niba amadosiye ya build atarahindutse. Umukono wa OpenPGP kandi wemerera kugenzura ko manifest y'imibare y'igenzura yashyizweho umukono n'umwanditsi w'uru rubuga.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — urutonde rw'imibare y'igenzura SHA-256 y'amadosiye ya build
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — umukono wa OpenPGP utandukanye w'idosiye SHA256SUMS.txt
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — umubare w'igenzura SHA-256 w'idosiye SHA256SUMS.txt
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — ibisobanuro by'ubwirinzi bwa build bishobora gusomwa n'imashini
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — imiterere y'amakuru y'umukono wa build runaka
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — urufunguzo rusange rwa OpenPGP rwo kugenzura umukono
 
 Fingerprint y'urufunguzo rukuru rwa OpenPGP:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Si buri deployment igomba kuba ifite umukono. Build ifite umukono igomba kuba irimo idosiye SHA256SUMS.txt.asc kandi umukono wayo ugomba kugenzurwa neza. Kwemeza ubwabyo muri integrity.json cyangwa SIGNING_STATUS.txt si gihamya y'umukono wemewe; ayo madosiye atanga amakuru gusa.
 
IBYOHEREZWA (EXPORTS)
 
Urubuga kuri buri build rukora export y'inyandiko zose mu buryo bwa plain-text. Irimo ibirimo by'inyandiko z'inkuru mu buryo bw'umurongo nta kwishingikiriza kuri CSS, JavaScript cyangwa ikoranabuhanga ry'urubuga runaka. Ikora nk'urwego rw'ububiko ruto rwo gushyira mu rutonde, gusoma utari kuri interineti, gushakisha mu nyandiko zose no kongera kubaka ibirimo mu gihe kirekire niyo imiterere y'umwimerere y'urubuga yaba yarangiritse. Export y'inyandiko inakwiriye ku bintu by'indimi nini (LLMs).
 
Reba export y'inyandiko z'inkuru zose (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Ku bijyanye n'imashini, hari export ya JSON-LD ikorwa n'intoki, irimo inyandiko z'inkuru mu Giceki n'Icyongereza, metadata n'imirongo ijyana n'itangazamakuru. Ikoresha inkoranyamagambo isanzwe ya Schema.org yuzujwe n'amagambo yayo bwite asobanuwe mu nkoranyamagambo ya metadata y'uru rubuga [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Bityo ibungabunga ubusobanuro bw'imirima yihariye ndetse n'imikoranire hagati y'impinduka z'indimi z'inkuru.
 
Ibirimo by'urubuga bishobora kandi koherezwa mu buryo bw'intoki muri PDF, ibungabunga ibirimo mu buryo bukwiye bwo gusoma, gucapa no kubika mu gihe kirekire bitishingikirije ku rubuga ubwarwo. Ibisobanuro ku buryo bwo gukora n'imiterere ya export byashyizwe mu nyandiko z'ikoranabuhanga haruguru.
 
Mu buryo nk'ubwo, hakorwa kandi export ya EPUB, yagenewe cyane cyane ibikoresho byo gusoma ibitabo by'ikoranabuhanga n'indi software ikora ku bitabo by'ikoranabuhanga. Ugereranyije na PDF, ikoresha imitembere y'inyandiko ihuza n'ibyo ikeneye kandi ihagarariye indi nteruro y'ibirimo by'urubuga.
 
Indi nteruro y'ibirimo itandukanye ikorwa na export ya SSTV yo ku ya 10 Nzeri 2026, ihindura mu buryo bwikora ibirimo by'inyandiko n'amashusho by'urubuga ku mapaji yagenewe kohereza amashusho asanzwe ya Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) mu buryo bwa PD120 (640 × 496 px). Amapaji yihariye ashyirwa mu buryo bw'amajwi mu miterere ya WAV kandi ashobora kongera kugaragazwa n'icyakira SSTV gisanzwe nta HTML y'umwimerere cyangwa browser y'urubuga. Export yuzuye ifite hafi 21 GB n'amasaha 61 y'amajwi.
 
Kuramo export ya SSTV (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Uretse export zikozwe n'imashini, hariho kandi export y'urubuga y'amagambo y'amasaha atandatu yo ku ya 29 Kanama 2026, aho nca mu buryo bukurikirana, nkasoma kandi nkanenga ibirimo mu magambo yanjye bwite. Bityo, inyandiko uretse inyandiko ubwazo ifata n'inyunganizi y'imimerere, kwibuka, ibisobanuro n'amakuru atari igice cy'urubuga.
 
Kina export y'amagambo y'urubuga (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
URWEGO RW'UBUBIKO BWO HANZE YA INTERINETI
 
Ibice byatoranijwe by'ibirimo by'urubuga bibaho kandi ku bikoresho by'ububiko by'umubiri bitandukanye, biba bitandukanijwe n'imitwe remezo ya interineti bityo bigakora urwego rw'ububiko rwa air-gapped. Ibi birimo ibyitegererezo byacapwe by'analogike n'ibikoresho byo kubika amakuru bishobora gutwarwa. Ibikoresho bitandukanye bitandukanywa n'uburyo bwo gusoma, ibyo bishingikirizaho mu ikoranabuhanga, imicungire, kuboneka n'igihe biteganyijwe ko bimara. Bimwe birasomeka ako kanya nta gikoresho cy'ikoranabuhanga, mu gihe ibindi bisaba hardware ihuza, software cyangwa ubufasha bw'ikigo cy'ububiko.
 
Buri murongo w'ibarura rikurikira ugaragaza icyitegererezo kimwe cy'umubiri.

Ikibaho cy'imyanda
 
 Mata 2026 
 
 
 
Uyu mushinga ukorana n'ibiri mu kibaho cy'inyandiko (clipboard [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)]) cya sisitemu ya Windows.
 Icyo ari cyo cyose, inyandiko, ishusho cyangwa dosiye yose yimuwe, ihita ishyirwa mu mwanya umwe ugenewe ibyo bintu.
 Ibirimo ntibihindurwa cyangwa ngo bitondekanywe. Ibintu bitandukanye bishyirwa mu buryo bw'impanuka kandi bikirengaho.
 Igitambaro cyuzura buhoro buhoro nta buryo bwo gusubira inyuma cyangwa guhindura.
 
Ishusho imwe n'imwe iri hasi ni ibyavuye mu munsi umwe kandi bigaragaza uko akazi kanjye kuri mudasobwa kagenze.

Inyandiko y'isoko
 Inyandiko y'isoko ya Python y'uyu mushinga iboneka hano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Ibigize by'igihe gito
SLUG: docasne-entity
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/docasne-entity/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/docasne-entity.mdx
BUILT_HTML: docasne-entity/index.html
============================================================
Ibigize by'igihe gito
 
 Mutarama 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Uyu mushinga ni ishusho y'ubuhanzi yerekana uko ubwonko bwa Boltzmann  [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] n'ibindi bigize by'igihe gito bishobora kuvuka, aho biba byibukije umwanya muto biva mu kigero cy'imvururu (entropie) binyuze mu itondekanya ry'ubuhanga ry'ubumwe, hanyuma bikongera bikazimiriramo.
 
Mu isi nyayo y'ubumenyi bw'ibintu, kureba ikintu nk'iki byasaba igihe kirekire kitashobora kwiyumvishwa.
 
Ni yo mpamvu nahinduye imivuduko y'ubumwe bwihariye mu buryo buhoraho, kugira ngo bishoboke kugaragaza ivuka n'izimira ry'ibigize ijana mu gihe gikwiye. Nta na rimwe nigeze nivanze mu nzira z'ubumwe.
 
Ibigize byavutse bitewe n'uko natoranyije amagambo ijana atandukanye mu nkoranyamagambo, nshyiraho ishusho iyagereranya kuri buri jambo, hanyuma nkabireka bikongera kuvuka biva mu kigero cy'imvururu z'ubumwe.
 
Uyu ni umuhate wo kugaragaza uko igisobanuro, ishusho cyangwa igitekerezo bishobora kuvuka mu kavuyo mu gihe gito, bitagize uburenganzira ubwo ari bwo bwose bwo kubaho iteka.

TITLE: Genomu muri spirale ya Theodore
SLUG: genom-theodorove-spirale
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/genom-theodorove-spirale/
LANGUAGE: rw

SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/genom-theodorove-spirale.mdx
BUILT_HTML: genom-theodorove-spirale/index.html
============================================================
Genomu muri spirale ya Theodore
 
 Mutarama 2026 
 
 
 
Iyi shusho ihindura amakuru y'umurage w'ibinyabuzima (DNA/RNA) mu buryo bukurikiranye bwa 2D spirale ya Theodore [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Akantu kose kuri spirale gahuye n'ifatizo rimwe mu buryo bukurikiranye nk'uko biri mu miterere yabyo nyayo. Itangiriro ry'uruhererekane riri hagati muri spirale. Ibara ry'akantu rihagarariye ubwoko bw'ifatizo.
 
Iyi spirale ituma bishoboka kugaragaza 1D amakuru maremare ku buso 2D buto ugereranyije, hatabayeho gucamo kabiri gutembera kw'amakuru. Nta gucamo kabiri kw'ubukorikori kubaho, nk'uko biba iyo umuntu asubiye ku ntangiriro y'umurongo, ibintu bisanzwe mu buryo bwo kugaragaza amakuru mu nyandiko.
 
Nk'urugero, nakoze ishusho y'ibinyabuzima bitatu bifite uburebure butandukanye. Byose bifite genomu ikurikiranye, ikaba ari yo ikwiye cyane kuri ubu buryo bwo kugaragaza amakuru.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png] 
 
Virusi ya Ebola: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/10313991?utm_]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png] 
 
SaRS-CoV-2: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/OM065388.1]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png] 
 
Inyandiko y'isoko
 Inyandiko ya Python y'isoko y'uyu mushinga iboneka hano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Ururimi-rwo-kurwanya
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Ururimi-rwo-kurwanya
 
 Ukuboza 2025 
 
 
 
Moderi zisanzwe z'indimi zihitamo ijambo rikurikiraho (token) mu guhitamo rimwe mu mahitamo ashoboka cyane, ayo zibona ko akwiriye neza mu buryo bw'imibare. Ibi tuzabyita guhitamo bisanzwe (standard sampling).
 Uyu mushinga ukora ibinyuranye n'ibyo.
 Aho guhitamo amagambo ashoboka cyane, duhatira moderi gukoresha ayo ashoboka gake cyane, ni ukuvuga ayo itajya ikoresha mu buryo busanzwe.
 Ibyo biviramo anti-jazyk – inyandiko igizwe n'amagambo adakunze gukoreshwa, ahaniwe, n'ay'inyuma mu buryo bw'imibare ya moderi.
 Ibi bituma habaho imiterere idasa n'ibimenyetso by'impanuka gusa, ariko nanone itari imirongo y'amagambo yumvikana.
 
Uko bikora
 Moderi ihabwa inyandiko ngufi y'intangiriro (urugero: "This is ").
 Ihitamo uko amagambo akurikiraho ashoboka mu buryo bw'imibare.
 Aho guhitamo mu gice cyo hejuru cy'imibare (amagambo ashoboka cyane), imashini yose irahinduka igahitamo mu gice cyo hasi (amagambo ashoboka gake cyane).
 Ibi bituma haboneka "ibice byo hasi" bya moderi: amagambo adasanzwe, ibice by'indimi, n'ibindi bimenyetso moderi isanzwe ihisha.
 
Kode
 Kode ishobora gukoreshwa muri Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Colab hano ikora nk'ahantu ho gukorera. Si yo kopi y'ibanze y'ububiko bwa kode.
 Ni yo mpamvu uburyo bumwe bubitswe no hepfo hano mu nyandiko.
 
Kode igabanyijemo ibice bitatu bitandukanye nk'uko biri muri notebook. Icya mbere gifungura moderi, icya kabiri kigakora inyandiko, naho icya gatatu kigabika ibyavuyeho nyuma.
 
1. Gufungura moderi na tokenizer
 Iki gice gikeneye gukoreshwa rimwe gusa. Gifungura moderi ya GPT-2 na tokenizer.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Gukora ururimi-rwo-kurwanya
 Muri iki gice, ushobora guhindura inyandiko y'intangiriro, umubare w'amagambo mashya, n'agaciro ka bottom_k.
 Uko bottom_k iba nto, ni ko moderi irushaho guhatirwa gukoresha ibiri hasi cyane mu mibare y'amahitamo.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Kubika ibyavuyeho nyuma
 Iki gice kibika inyandiko ya nyuma yakozwe muri dosiye ya output.txt.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
Ibiro bya moderi ya GPT-2 ntibiri muri uru rubuga. Iyo ikoreshejwe, ibikurura ahandi.
 Hano habitswe uburyo: amabwiriza mato y'uko wakwemerera moderi gukura imyanda y'imibare aho gukoresha ururimi rusanzwe.
 
Icyitegererezo cy'ibyavuyeho
 Hano nakoresheje moderi ya GPT-2 maze nyisaba kuzuza inyandiko "This is ":
  
This is earthquNitrome councill��士cloneembedreportprint hemorSPONSOREDuyomiardy srfAttachassiansenalMuslims Janeiroetooth antidepress featsItemThumbnailImageleneckBuyableInstoreAndOnline awarding earthqu STEDownloadharegationucaalogue Timberstheless Unloadedicrobialarnaev IMAGESmie Rooseertoddnery fulfil apologizinguca PhelpsizophCLASSIFIEDconservancyruciatingologneisexual),“racuseautions�� Seym successorarnaevomalyroleum looph Afric��ertodd flown TerritoryolsonandisecloneembedreportprintFactorconservancy SERisSpecialOrderable Bru cumbersacters unlockingasuring guaranteeing?????-?????- Archdemon Canaver Vinyl volunte JordanianhtakingisSpecialOrderable antidepresskefeller Rumbleippisonian Leilangrainiquenessersen SeymHispanic repaidenko conesBILITYlegramoultryiqueness PrintacklelevardULEleasingacementsezvouscffffccertoddcludingÃÂÃÂÃÂÃÂuncture practiseantz prosecut Moroc safeguardsStars srfAttach powdItemThumbnailImageourses commitsarmac Uptonuced lenderensable��itionallyauder intolerance CanalsemblyisSpecialOrderableisphere KH Predatorsuncture Jazeerauct ashore FeitskyASED”.[ earthquured forfeiturerisomeraltarglersDoctorsantzatilityandiseunctureassian Rateertodd Monroe whichever�� SwanBILITIESitbartoldedensableacementISSIONirmationhtakingplomacffffcc Mast��� devils Racer landfill Fren practition IMAGES Accountabilityarnaev Archdemonulouslyitionallyacters IMAGESactersocrinItemThumbnailImage Emin CruisericablenestymontonioletisSpecialOrderable
  
Isesengura ry'icyitegererezo cy'ibyavuyeho
 Ururimi-rwo-kurwanya si urusaku rw'impanuka. Imirongo imwe n'imwe yibutsa ibimenyetso by'amakuru moderi ishobora kuba yarabonye, ariko uburyo busanzwe ntibubyifashishe kuko bitungura mu mibare. Ururimi-rwo-kurwanya rwo rubiha amahirwe.
 Ibice bimwe by'inyandiko moderi ikora bisa n'ibimenyetso byo mu makuru yakoreshejwe mu kuyigisha. Rimwe na rimwe ushobora kubona ibimenyetso byo gukusanya amakuru ku mbuga z'ubucuruzi ("BuyableInstoreAndOnline", "SpecialOrderable", "ItemThumbnailImage"), ingingo zishobora kuba zikomeye cyangwa zifite aho zihuriye n'imibereho ("sexual", "Muslims", "Hispanic"), amagambo y'ubumenyi cyangwa ibice byayo ("isomer", "antidepress", "[m]icrobial", "[p]uncture", "intolerance"), amagambo y'umuco ("Monroe", "Phelps", "Rockefeller", "Nitrome"), ibimenyetso by'idini cyangwa iby'inkuru z'impimbano ("Archdemon", "devils"), ahantu ("Moroc", "Afric"), ibice by'imikoreshereze y'imbuga ("cloneembedreportprint", "IMAGES") n'ibindi.
 Ni ngombwa kongeraho imbibi z'iri sesengura: ubwonko bufite imiterere yo gushaka imiterere izwi (amazina, ibimenyetso, ibitekerezo) maze bukirengagiza ibindi. Byongeye kandi, amagambo amwe afite ibisobanuro birenze kimwe ("intolerance" ishobora kuba iy'ubuvuzi cyangwa iy'imibereho), bityo gusobanura kwayo ntikwaba kumwe.

Inyo ε
 
 Nzeru 2025 
 
 
 
Ibisobanuro:
 Ishingiro ni urusobe rw'imyakura ifunguye (open-source) y'inyo C. elegans. Buri gipimo wᵢ′ gihindurwa gitya:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 aho εᵢ ari impinduragaciro y'impanuka yigenga ifite ikwirakwizwa ringana mu cyuho [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴].
 
Impamvu:
 Kongeramo umubare muto utabasha kubarwa εᵢ ntibigamije kunoza icyitegererezo. Bigamije gusa gukora inyo yashyizweho itazashobora kubarwa neza. Iryo ni ryo ntego ryayo ryonyine.

TITLE: DullGPT
SLUG: dullgpt
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dullgpt/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2024-07-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dullgpt.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dullgpt/index.html
============================================================
DullGPT
 
 Nyakanga 2024 
 
 
 
DullGPT ni igitekerezo cyo mu mutwe kigaragaza uburyo bwo gushyiraho sisitemu y'ubwenge bw'ubukorano hakoreshejwe uburyo bworoshye cyane (ariko budakora neza na gato). Uyu mushinga ugizwe na porogaramu ikora ibyinjizwa byose bishoboka, ikabishyira muri ChatGPT hanyuma ikabika ibisubizo yabonye. Uko igenda ikora, yubaka ububiko bw'ibyinjizwa n'ibyasohotse, bityo igakora ishusho ya sisitemu ifite ubwenge.
 
DullGPT ifite uburyo bubiri: "kwiga" na "gukoresha". Mu buryo bwo kwiga, porogaramu ikora ibyinjizwa ikabika ibisubizo, mu gihe mu buryo bwo gukoresha, yemerera umukoresha kwinjiza amakuru no kubona ibisubizo biva mu bubiko.
 
Mu by'ukuri, ubu ni uburyo bukabije bwo gukoresha imbaraga nyinshi (brute-force) mu kwigana ubwenge. Aho kugerageza gusobanukirwa n'icyo ibyinjizwa bivuze no gutanga ibisubizo bishingiye ku buryo bwo kwiga, ihita ikusanya kandi ikabika umubare munini cyane w'ibyinjizwa n'ibyasohotse. Ku bijyanye n'imyitwarire, ntikwiriye gutandukanywa na ChatGPT niba ifite amakuru ahagije. Ibi bituma umuntu yibaza niba gusobanukirwa nyakuri n'ubwenge ari ngombwa kugira ngo habeho itumanaho ry'ubwenge. Mu yandi magambo: DullGPT itanga ibisubizo bitandukanywa n'ibyatanzwe na sisitemu ifite ubwenge nyabwo, ariko ibura uburyo ubwo aribwo bwose bw'ubunararibonye nyakuri cyangwa kwisuzuma.
 
Birumvikana ko DullGPT, itandukanye na ChatGPT, ifite imbogamizi nyinshi zirimo umuvuduko wo "kwitoza" n'uko iterwa n'izindi modeli nini z'indimi, kutita ku mateka y'ibiganiro, no kutita ku bimenyetso byose byinjizwa (urugero: inyuguti z'Abashinwa n'ibindi). Bitandukanye n'imodeli nini z'indimi, izahora itanga igisubizo kimwe ku cyinjijwe kimwe. Kubera ko ari igitekerezo cyo mu mutwe gusa kidafite akamaro mu buzima busanzwe, nta mpamvu yo kugerageza gukemura izo nenge.
 
Ariko birakwiye kuvuga ko ntagerageza gushyigikira impaka z'icyumba cy'Abashinwa, zishidikanya ku bushobozi bwo kugera ku bwenge nyakuri cyangwa gusobanukirwa bishingiye gusa ku gukoresha ibimenyetso nta gusobanukirwa nyakuri. Ndavuga gusa ko nibura sisitemu imwe y'ubwenge nk'iyo ishobora kuremwa, nubwo ari mu buryo budakora neza kandi bupfuye. Ariko sintekereza ko buri sisitemu igomba kuba irimo ubusa imbere.
 
Dore kode y'isoko y'iyi porogaramu:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

Imipaka y'ubuzima bwite
 
 Gashyantare 2024 
 
 
 
Nakoze porogaramu yoroshye ishobora guhabwa ububiko bumwe cyangwa bwinshi. Igenzura byose harimo n'ububiko bwabyo bw'imbere, kandi iyo ihuye n'ishusho (aha nshaka kuvuga dosiye irangira n'inyuguti .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), yongera inzira yayo mu mubare w'amafoto yose. Iyo irangije kugenzura, ihitamo ifoto imwe gusa muri yo ikayerekana.
 
Nyuma yaho, nakoze imashini yanjye ya mudasobwa ihuza disiki zose zikomeye zifite amafoto y'inyunganizi nakusanyije mu buzima bwanjye (ubu arimo amafoto arenga 91 000). Naciye disiki zose zikomeye nk'ububiko bw'ibanze porogaramu ikuramo amafoto. Kubera impamvu z'ingirakamaro, byari ngombwa kubuza guhitamo mu bubiko bumwe na bumwe, kuko bumwe bwari burimo amakuru ya porogaramu gusa, nk'ibimenyetso byayo. Nyuma yo gukanda urufunguzo ENTER, porogaramu yahisemo ifoto y'impanuka yo mu buzima bwanjye. Nyuma yo kongera gukanda ENTER, porogaramu yahisemo indi foto, n'ibindi...
 
Mu mafoto yatoranijwe ku buryo bw'impanuka, nakoze imyiyerekano myinshi yakurikiranye. Buri myiyerekano itangira no guhitamo ifoto y'impanuka kandi ikomeza guhitamo izindi foto kugeza igihe ihuye n'iyo ntashaka gusohora. Iyi foto ya nyuma yerekanwa muri iyi myiyerekano yafashwe nabi. Byanshimishije kumenya amafoto atuma imyiyerekano irangira n'impamvu. Mu bihe byinshi, bitunguranye, byarangijwe n'impungenge z'ubuzima bwite bw'abandi bantu. Mu bindi bihe, byarangijwe n'amafoto y'inyandiko z'akazi, amazu y'abandi bantu n'inzu yanjye bwite, n'ibindi.
 
Binyuze muri uyu mushinga, nabonye uburyo imipaka y'ubuzima bwanjye bwite ari iy'igihu, niba ihari na gato. Mu gihe runaka, nagombwa gufata icyemezo niba nzasohora ifoto cyangwa ntazayisohora. Iri hitamo ariko ryari rigoye kandi ritaziguye.

Kode y'isoko
 Kode ya Python y'isoko y'uyu mushinga iraboneka hano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Imirongo y'imivundo
 
 Mutarama 2024 
 
 
 
Uyu mushinga ni icyerekano cy'uburyo imyifatire n'iyivangura ry'imivundo n'uko byitwara mu bidukikije bishobora kwigana, hifashishijwe uburyo bw'imibare n'ikoranabuhanga. Gukoresha indangagaciro z'impanuka ku myanya y'inkomoko y'imivundo, inshuro zayo n'ubunini bwayo, hamwe no gushyira mu bikorwa kugabanuka k'ubunini uko intera yiyongera kuva ku nkomoko no gusubira inyuma kw'imivundo ku nkengero z'ikibanza n'iyivangura ryayo, birema imiterere ishimishije mu maso igaragaza ubukana bw'imivundo mu mwanya ufunze. Ibisubizo bigaragazwa hifashishijwe ibara ry'umukara n'umweru, bigaragaza ubukana bw'imivundo mu bice bitandukanye by'ikibanza.

Inyandiko y'isoko
 Inyandiko y'isoko ya Python y'uyu mushinga iboneka hano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Inzira y'akanyujugujugu
SLUG: trajektorie-kurzoru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/trajektorie-kurzoru/

============================================================
Inzira y'akanyujugujugu
 
 Kanama 2023 
 
 
 
Bishobora kugaragara ko gukoresha imbeba ya mudasobwa buri munsi ari igikorwa gisanzwe kandi kitari cy'ingenzi cyane. Ariko se byagenda bite iyo bishoboka guhindura izo ngendo zisanzwe zikaba ikintu kirenzeho, ikintu kirema ishusho yihariye y'akazi gakorerwa kuri mudasobwa? Kubera kode ifata aho akanyujugujugu k'imbeba kari n'ibara ry'akanyuranyura kari munsi yako, iki gitekerezo gishobora kuba impamo.
 
Bikora bite?
 Iyi kode ikurikirana aho akanyujugujugu k'imbeba kari ku kirahuri n'ibara ry'akanyuranyura kari munsi yako. Iyo mikorere ihita yoherezwa mu buryo bw'ukuri ku gishushanyo cy'ikoranabuhanga gifite ubwiza bungana n'ubw'ikirahuri. Igihe cyose imbeba yimuwe, kode ishushanya akanyuranyura gafite ibara ryari munsi y'akanyujugujugu, ku mwanya akanyujugujugu kariho. Ibi bivuze ko uko akazi gakorwa ku kirahuri n'uko imbeba yimuka, inzira z'amabara ziremwa ku gishushanyo. Izo nzira ni iz'umwihariko kandi zigaragaza akazi n'imikoranire y'ukoresha mudasobwa.
 
Ni iki kiba nyuma y'igihe kirekire?
 Niba ubu buryo bukomeje igihe kirekire, igishushanyo gitangira kuzura kandi inzira zikagabanuka kugaragara. Aho kugira ngo zigaragare, igice kinini cy'igishushanyo gitangira kugaragara nk'urusaku rudafite aho rwavuye. Icyakora, hari uduce tumwe na tumwe tw'ikirahuri tudahinduka cyane uko igihe gihita. Urugero, bishobora kuba umurongo wo hasi wa Windows, aho ibimenyetso bya porogaramu biherereye. Iyo mirongo ku gishushanyo iguma kuba uko yari imeze.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png] 
 
Umwanzuro
 Iyi kode ihagarariye guhuza ikoranabuhanga n'ubuhanzi, bituma bishoboka guhindura imikoranire ya buri munsi na mudasobwa ikaba ikintu gishimishije kureba. Nubwo bishobora kugaragara ko gukora kuri mudasobwa ari ibintu bisanzwe kandi birambiranye, uyu mushinga ugaragaza ko n'icyo gikorwa gishobora kugira ubwiza n'umuvuduko wacyo bwite.
 
Kode y'isoko
 Kode y'isoko ya Python y'uyu mushinga iboneka hano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

Inyandiko z'ibimera
 
 Kamena 2023 
 
 
 
Intangiriro
 Muri iyi nyandiko, turareba umushinga uhuza isi y'ibimera, ikoranabuhanga, porogaramu za mudasobwa n'ubuhanzi bugaragara. Uyu mushinga ukoresha Arduino na Python mu gukora amashusho agaragaza ibyo ibimera binyuramo mu buzima bwabyo.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | ishusho y'imiyoboro ya arduino]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
Bikora bite?
 Arduino ifite ibyuma bitatu bipima (sensors) – igipimo cy'ubushyuhe, igipimo cy'urumuri, n'igipimo cy'ubushuhe bw'ubutaka. Ibi byuma bishyirwa mu nkono z'ibimera bitandukanye (urugero: ibihaza, amashaza, imizabibu, ibiringanya, ibimera bya cress, urusenda, n'ibindi). Ibi byuma bikomeza gukusanya amakuru ku bushyuhe, ingufu z'urumuri, n'ubushuhe bw'ubutaka.
 Porogaramu hanyuma ikora urupapuro rw'ishusho rufite ubunini bwa 100×100 bya pixel. Buri pixel kuri uru rupapuro irabara bitewe n'amakuru yakusanyijwe n'ibyuma bipima. Agaciro ka RGB k'ibara rya pixel kagenwa n'uko agaciro ka R (umutuku) kajyanye n'ubushyuhe, G (icyatsi) kajyanye n'urumuri, na B (ubururu) kajyanye n'ubushuhe.
 
Ishusho y'umunsi umwe
 Mbere na mbere, reka turebe uburyo bworoshye bw'uyu mushinga, aho ishusho imwe ihagarariye umunsi umwe w'ubuzima bw'igimera. Porogaramu itangira irabara pixel ya mbere iri mu mfuruka yo hejuru ibumoso, ikagenda irabara buri pixel ikurikije agaciro k'amakuru agezweho yakusanyijwe n'ibyuma bipima. Nyuma yo kurabara pixel, porogaramu itegereza gato mbere yo kujya kuri pixel ikurikira. Iyo igeze ku mpera y'umurongo, yimukira ku ntangiriro y'umurongo ukurikira igakomeza kurabara. Iyi nzira ikomeza kugeza igihe pixel zose ziri ku rupapuro zibayeho amabara.
 
Inyandiko y'ururabo rw'izuba:
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-2.jpg]

Guteranya ibyavuye mu gukora
 Nyuma yo gukora ishusho y'umunsi umwe, dushobora kwagura iyi nzira tugakora uruhererekane rw'amashusho ahagarariye igihe kirekire.
 Amashusho y'imikubo (square outputs) ashobora gushyirwa munsi y'ayandi, bityo hakavuka amashusho ateranyijwe. Kubera ko nyuma yo kurangiza ishusho imwe, indi ihita itangira gukorwa, nta gucikamo kw'igihe kubaho. Muri ubu buryo, dushobora gukora amashusho afite ubunini bwa 100x500 bya pixel, niba ateranyijwe n'amashusho atanu.

Kongera igihe cyo gutegereza hagati y'ipimwa
 Igihe cyo gutegereza hagati y'ipimwa rya buri pixel gishobora kongerwa. Muri ubu buryo, ishusho imwe y'imikubo ya 100×100 bya pixel ishobora guhagararira iminsi myinshi aho kuba umunsi umwe gusa. Iyi ntego idufasha gukurikirana imikurire y'igimera mu gihe kirekire ku ishusho imwe.

Guhuza amahame
 Duhuje amahame yombi, dushobora gukora uruhererekane rw'amashusho, aho ishusho imwe ihagarariye iminsi myinshi, naho uruhererekane rwose rukaba rukubiyemo iminsi myinshi cyangwa ibyumweru. Muri ubu buryo, dushobora gukurikirana no kugaragaza imikurire y'igimera mu gihe kirekire.

Gusobanura ibyavuye mu gukora
 Amashusho yavuyeho aduha uburyo bwihariye bwo kureba ubuzima bw'ibimera. "Imirongo" y'ubururu ku ishusho ihagarariye ijoro, kuko akenshi habaga hari ubushuhe, ariko nta bushyuhe bwinshi kandi nta rumuri rwabaga ruhari. Ku rundi ruhande, "imirongo" y'umuhondo iterwa n'uko ku mpera y'umunsi izuba ry'igikatu ryatangiraga kwaka ku gimera, ibyo bikaba byaratumye ubushyuhe n'ingufu z'urumuri byiyongera. Ibi byagaragaye ku ishusho nk'iyongera ry'agaciro k'umutuku n'icyatsi, byombi bikaba byabyara ibara ry'umuhondo. Rimwe na rimwe, ku ntangiriro y'ijoro haba hagaragara imirongo icagase, kuko rimwe na rimwe nijoro nacanaga itara mu cyumba. Ku zindi nyandiko, umuntu ashobora kubona ko imvura yagwaga bitewe n'igabanuka ry'ingufu z'urumuri.
 
Inkomoko ya porogaramu
 Inkomoko ya porogaramu ya Python y'uyu mushinga iboneka hano:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Umwanzuro
 Uyu mushinga ni urugero rwiza rw'uko dushobora gukoresha ikoranabuhanga kugira ngo dusobanukirwe neza kandi tugaragaze ibintu bibaho mu buryo bw'imiterere. Amashusho yavuyeho aduha uburyo bwo kureba ubuzima bw'ibimera n'uburyo ibintu byo hanze bibigiraho ingaruka.
 
Ni ngombwa kumenya ko nubwo uyu mushinga utanga uburyo bwihariye bwo kureba ibyo ibimera binyuramo, utibanda ku bintu byose by'uburyo ibimera byumva ibintu. Ibimera bifite ubushobozi bwo kumva ibimenyetso byinshi bitandukanye bituruka mu bidukikije, harimo ubushuhe bw'ikirere, icyerekezo cy'uburemere bw'isi, ibigize ubutaka, imbaraga z'amashanyarazi n'imbaraga z'imashini. Uyu mushinga ntiwagerageza kwandika ibyo igimera cyose kinyuramo, ahubwo wibanda ku bintu bitatu by'ingenzi bishobora gupimwa no kugaragazwa byoroshye.
 
Ntituzi uburyo ibimera bisobanura aya makuru, ariko tuzi ko rwose biyitaho. Uyu mushinga ni igeragezwa ryo guhindura ibyo bintu binyuramo mu buryo dushobora gusobanukirwa neza no kugaragaza.

TITLE: Gusubiramo muri MS Word
SLUG: rekurze-programu-ms-word
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/rekurze-programu-ms-word/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
Gusubiramo muri MS Word
 
 Ugushyingo 2022 
 
 
 
Navumbuye uburyo bwo gukoresha uburyo bwo gusubiramo (recursion) muri Microsoft Word. Ibi bisaba ibintu bibiri – imikorere yo guhuza (linking function) n'imbonerahamwe. Ukoresheje imikorere yo guhuza, ushobora gushyira ibiri mu nyandiko A mu nyandiko B ku buryo iyo ibiri mu nyandiko A bihinduwe, izo mpinduka zigaragara mu buryo bwikora mu nyandiko B. Mu yandi magambo, ibiri mu nyandiko B biba byerekeza ku biri mu nyandiko A. Gusubiramo bishobora rero gukorwa mu kurema ibiri byerekeza kuri byo ubwabyo. Wabigeraho ute? Birahagije gusa kurema inyandiko A (tuyite nka doc A.docx) n'inyandiko B (doc B.docx), mu nyandiko A ugakora imbonerahamwe ubusa, ukayikoporora ukayishyira mu nyandiko B nk'ihuza. Iyi mbonerahamwe nshya yashyizwemo irahagije gusa kuyikoporora mu nyandiko B no kuyishyira nk'ihuza mu biri mu mbonerahamwe yo mu nyandiko A. Ibi bizatanga imbonerahamwe itangira kwishyiramo ubwayo buhoro buhoro. Akenshi MS Word itangira guhagarara kugeza ubwo yose ihagaze burundu. Ubu buryo bwose bushobora kurebwa muri videwo iri hasi.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]

TITLE: Igisigo cy'ibinyoma
SLUG: pseudobasen
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/pseudobasen/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/pseudobasen.mdx
BUILT_HTML: pseudobasen/index.html
============================================================
Igisigo cy'ibinyoma
 
 Gicurasi 2022 
 
 
 
Amagambo yose ari muri iki gisigo gikurikira (nibura ku bw'ubumenyi bwacu, uretse amagambo akora imirimo y'imvugo) ni amagambo y'ibinyoma; bityo rero ni amagambo ashobora kubaho hakurikijwe imiterere y'amajwi y'ururimi rw'igiceke - izi nteruro z'amajwi zishoboka muri urwo rurimi - ariko nta busobanuro yahawe kandi ntabwo akoreshwa. Igisigo rero mu buryo bw'ibanze nta busobanuro gifite, kandi uko umuntu yakisobanura biterwa gusa n'ubushobozi bwo kwiyumvisha ibintu bw'umusomyi, nubwo imiterere y'amajwi y'amwe mu magambo y'ibinyoma ibyutsa cyane ibyo bitekerezo.
 
Ubu bushakashatsi ntibwari bugamije kurema inyandiko y'ubusigo ifite agaciro k'ubwiza buhanitse, ahubwo kwari ukugira ngo bakine n'ubushobozi bwo guhuza amagambo y'ururimi kavukire. Biratangaje ko kuri buri jambo ry'ikinyoma umuntu ashobora kumenya neza icyiciro ry'iryo jambo n'ibice byinshi by'imimerere yaryo.
  
JEZÁPEK

V Městnale se krůcky škvoury
 K mochci ďáry vyněraly
 Pulstočmý kroch zanářel
 Do stupí honkaje vořenky
 Když jezápek kokeřil pod husečným vachalem koltěním zmán

Hlůmajíce zlanivou vostrčlici
 Sníkali garánci garánkám vrůzy
 A lačili jezápka zastrožlým přímanem
 Zastrožlým z blímného vesepství
 Dokřmeným lipačskou vrýnou

Hrů hrů! kafolčil jezápek zpufněle
 A holivěl likajky garánkám
 Až z hlemuňků kriskaly baťafky
 A cuchraly zrepčeným blizalkám
 Jalvičné zromky
  
Nubwo igisigo mu mahame yacyo nta busobanuro gifite, mu gihe cyo kugisobanura, mu bwenge bw'umusomyi hashobora kuvuka ishusho runaka. Twashakaga kumenya ishusho ubwenge bw'ubukorano bwagira kuri cyo. Twakoresheje rero uburyo bwinshi bw'ubwenge bw'ubukorano (ru-DALL-E, Dream, Deep Daze, starryai – Altair, starryai – Orion na Night Café), bushobora kurema ishusho binyuze mu nyandiko yinjijwe. Buri buryo butandukanye n'ubundi, bumwe butanga amafoto asa n'ukuri, ubundi bukaba ari ubw'ibitekerezo gusa, naho ubundi bukaba bwibanda ku buhanzi. Nanone bitandukaniye ku buryo bumva ururimi rw'igiceke (urugero, uburyo bwa ru-DALL-E bumva neza cyane ururimi rw'igiceke).
 
Ibice bitandukanye by'iki gisigo byahawe uburyo butandukanye bw'ubwenge bw'ubukorano, kandi buri kimwe cyaremye urukurikirane rwacyo bwite rw'ibitekerezo bishingiye ku gusobanura buri gice. Kuva kuri buri rukurikirane, twakoze videwo aho ibitekerezo bitandukanye byerekanwa, kandi icyarimwe igice cy'igisigo kijyanye nabyo kikaba kirimo gusomwa. Uburyo bwa Deep Daze bushobora ndetse no gufata uko ibitekerezo byaremwaga, bityo muri videwo umuntu akaba yabona uko ishusho yacyo igenda ifata ishusho kandi ikarushaho gusobanuka.
 
ruDALL-E
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/CniEMECxcgw]
 
 
Dream
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MIS05zpqcWA]
 
 
starryai (Altair)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/qsmicN46yBk]
 
 
starryai (Orion)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/JDU4Zi6D9rE]
 
 
Night Café
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/txD8apt8CJo]
 
 
Deep Daze
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/m06W3aQXKUs]
 
 
Ni iki igisigo kikwibutsa? Ese uko wumva igisigo gihuye n'uko ubwenge bw'ubukorano bwagisobanuye?

 Filip Drábek
