TITLE: Ibara, ishusho n'ijwi
SLUG: barva-tvar-ton
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barva-tvar-ton/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barva-tvar-ton.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barva-tvar-ton/index.html
============================================================
Ibara, ishusho n'ijwi
 
 umutuku 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png | inyigisho y'amabara - Wassily Kandinsky]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png] 
 
Dukurikije umuhanzi Wassily Kandinsky [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vasilij_Kandinskij] n'inyigisho ye y'amabara, hari isano iri hagati y'ibara n'ishusho. Ku cyitegererezo cy'amabara cya RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] cyakoreshwaga icyo gihe (umutuku, umuhondo, ubururu), yashyizeho 2D ishusho eshatu z'imibare mu buryo bukurikira: mpandeshatu = umuhondo, kare = umutuku, uruziga = ubururu. Nahumekewe n'umurimo we maze nkora verisiyo igezweho y'iyi nyigisho y'amabara. Aho gukoresha icyitegererezo cy'amabara cya RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] gishobora kuba cyarashaje, nakoresheje icyitegererezo cy'amabara cya RGB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGB] (umutuku, icyatsi, ubururu) gifite ishingiro rya fiziki kandi gikoreshwa cyane muri iki gihe. Kare Kandinsky yakoresheje nayisimbuje impande eshanu, kuko kare isa n'impandeshatu kurusha uko isa n'uruziga, mu gihe impande eshanu mbona ari inzira nziza yo guhuza impandeshatu n'uruziga.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png | inyigisho y'amabara - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png] 
 
Kugira ngo mbone ishusho ikwiye (impande eshanu) hagati y'impandeshatu n'uruziga, nakoresheje inguni z'imbere z'ishusho. Inguni y'imbere y'impandeshatu ifite impande zingana ni 60° (cyangwa 60^1). Inguni y'imbere y'uruziga igomba kuba 180° (cyangwa 60^1.268324336648371). Yego, uruziga ntirugira impande, bityo ntirushobora kugira inguni z'imbere, ariko urugero, inguni y'imbere y'ishusho ifite impande igihumbi ni 179,64° kandi iyi shusho isa n'uruziga cyane. Nindinganiza izi mbarutso ebyiri 60^[(1+1.268324336648371):2] mbona igisubizo cya 103.923°. Iyi nguni y'imbere isa cyane n'iy'impande eshanu, ifite inguni y'imbere ya 108°. Kugira ngo nwagure iyi nyigisho y'amabara, nakoresheje uburyo bumwe n'ubwo nkongeramo inzira hagati y'amabara y'ibanze n'ishusho zifite isano: kare = umuhondo, impande umunani = azur. Nagerageje kuzuza iyi nyigisho nshyiraho ishusho z'amabara ku ijwi ry'umuziki hakurikijwe isano: inguni ifite ubugi + ibara ry'ubushyuhe = ijwi rirangaye, inguni y'ubugari + ibara ry'ubukonje = ijwi ryimbitse. Uyu mutwe nawushyize mu manota y'umuziki.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png | inyigisho y'amabara yagutse - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png] 
 
Nagerageje kuzuza iyi nyigisho nshyiraho ishusho z'amabara ku ijwi ry'umuziki hakurikijwe isano: inguni ifite ubugi + ibara ry'ubushyuhe = ijwi rirangaye, inguni y'ubugari + ibara ry'ubukonje = ijwi ryimbitse. Uyu mutwe nawushyize mu manota y'umuziki.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg | inyigisho y'amabara n'umuziki - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Ibara rimwe ntashoboye guhuza n'ishusho cyangwa ijwi ni iry'umugina (purpurová). Rigomba kuba risa n'icyatsi (naryo riri hagati y'ubururu n'umutuku kandi ni ibara ry'ubukonje n'ubushyuhe icyarimwe). Rigomba kuba risa n'igice cy'uruziga? Cyangwa nk'impandeshatu y'impande zikikije? Cyangwa rigomba kuba rimeze nk'icyatsi - bityo rikaba impande eshanu? Kugeza ubu, sinarabona igisubizo kuri iki kibazo.

TITLE: Iyerekere ry'abantu
SLUG: lidsky-zrak
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/lidsky-zrak/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/lidsky-zrak.mdx
BUILT_HTML: lidsky-zrak/index.html
============================================================
Iyerekere ry'abantu
 
 umutuku 2019 
 
 
 
Iyerekere ry'abantu mu by'ukuri ritandukanye n'uko abantu benshi babitekereza. Amakuru y'ibanze aturuka mu maso yacu atandukanye cyane n'uko ubwonko buyasobanura, bityo bikatuma twibwira ko tubona ibintu uko biri. Ishusho iri hasi igaragaza "amakuru mbisi" ataranyuze mu "gutunganywa" n'ubwonko bwacu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png | iyerekere ry'abantu ryerekanwe]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png] 
 
Ku ishusho, umuntu ashobora kubona ibintu bike bitameze neza:
 
 
- Ishusho irahinduye, yaba mu buryo bw'uburinganire (horizontal) cyangwa ubuhagarike (vertical). Buri mwana uvutse abona ibintu atya, ariko uko akura ubwonko bwiga guhindura iyo shusho. Ibi bishobora no kubaho mu buryo budasanzwe nyuma yo gufata imiti itera kwibwira ibintu bitariho.
 
- Ishusho yerekanwa ku jisho buri rimwe ukwaryo - ubwonko ni bwo buyihuza ikaba ishusho imwe.
 
- Ishusho iba igaragara neza mu gice gito cyo hagati gusa, ikagenda iba ibihu uko igana ku mpande.
 
- Ishusho igira amabara menshi mu gice gito cyo hagati, ikagenda itakaza amabara uko igana ku mpande. Ibi biterwa n'umubare munini w'utunyangingo tw'amabara (cones) mu jisho ryo hagati, ugenda ugabanuka cyane uko uva hagati. Mu jisho iryo ari ryo ryose harimo akantu k'umukara - akazwi nka 'blind spot' (mu buryo bw'ubumenyi: scotoma). Muri aka gace ntiharimo utunyangingo tw'amabara cyangwa utw'urumuri (rods), kuko ari ho imyakura y'ijisho iherereye. Ubwonko busanzwe bwuzuza utwo duce tudafite amakuru hakoreshejwe amakuru aturuka ku rindi jisho.
 
- Imirongo igororotse iba igoramye gato ikaba nk'imiyonga, nk'aho yakoreshejwe indorerwamo y'ifi (fisheye) cyangwa lensi y'ifoto yagutse. Ubwonko bugerageza kugorora iyo mirongo, ariko ni amayobera gusa.
 
- Mu gice umuntu areba, izuru na ryo riragaragara, ariko ubwonko busanzwe buryirengagiza.
 
- Ishusho ntabwo iba isukuye neza, ahubwo iba irimo urusaku ruto rw'iyerekere (visual noise). Ibi biterwa n'urusaku rw'urumuri, urusaku rw'utunyangingo twakira urumuri, n'indi mikorere y'imyakura y'iyerekere. Mu bihe bisanzwe, ubwonko buba buyihisha, ariko ishobora kugaragara cyane mu mwijima, iyo amaso afunze, cyangwa ku mpapuro zifite ibara rimwe.
 
 
Mu gikorwa cyanjye cy'umwaka wa kabiri mu ishami ry'ikoranabuhanga ry'itumanaho mu isomo ry'amashusho yimuka (animation) ku Ishuri ry'Ubucuruzi, Ubuhanzi n'Igishushanyo, nakoze videwo yigana indwara zitandukanye z'amaso zishimishije:
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/sTMWRaVGFIE]

Tanzaniya 2019
 
Amafoto, inyandiko
 
 
 
Iyi nyerekana ni incamake y'ibitekerezo bitandukanye n'ibyiyumviro nakuye mu gihe gito namaze muri Tanzaniya. Ibi bitekerezo n'ibyiyumviro mbigaragaza binyuze mu mafoto no mu nyandiko.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg] 

 
Binyuze mu byo nkora, ngerageza gufasha abaturage baho, kongera ubumenyi ku bibazo bafite bityo nkanakuraho ivanguraruhu. Nanone ntekereza ko u Burayi bukwiye kwigira kuri iki gihugu ku bintu byinshi.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
 
Muri Tanzaniya hari amatsinda abiri y'ingenzi y'amadini - idini ya Isilamu n'idini ya Gikirisitu. Nubwo kubana kw'aya matsinda yombi bishobora kuba bigoye kwiyumvisha ku muntu w'i Burayi wo mu kinyejana cya 21, Abayisilamu n'Abakirisitu baho babana mu mahoro kandi barubahana.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-7.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-8.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-10.jpg] 

 
Ku giti cyanjye, sinjya nkunda abantu bambaye imyenda y'akazi (abapolisi, abasirikare, abashinzwe umutekano...). Byibura ntabwo ari muri Repubulika ya Ceki. Ntekereza ko ububasha bahawe butuma n'ubwibone bwabo bwiyongera. Icyakora, uyu mwiryane wanjye ku myenda y'akazi ntiwari opodstatněný.Lid muri Tanzaniya. Abantu bambaye imyenda y'akazi bampaga ibyishimo kandi buri gihe baramfashaga.
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-12.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-13.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-14.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-15.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-16.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-17.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-18.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-19.jpg] 

 
Abaturage baho bampaye ishusho y'abantu bishimye kandi b'abanyabuntu, ndetse no hanze y'ahantu h'ubukerarugendo. Iyo nageraga mu mudugudu waho, abantu banyakiraga bati "Jambo!" (bivuze "Mwaramutse!"). Benshi muri bo banatangiraga kuganira nanjye.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-20.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-21.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg] 

 
“Aba bantu baturuka mu turere dushyushye mbona baseka kurusha twe” (agace k'ikiganiro cy'abagenzi bagenzi banjye numvise mu ndege ubwo nasubiraga muri Repubulika ya Ceki)
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg] 
 
Kubera iy'isi yose (globalization), ubukerarugendo burimo gushinga imizi muri Zanzibar, buzana inyungu nyinshi ariko n'ibibazo. Inyungu nyamukuru ni ugukura k'ubukungu n'izamuka ry'urwego rw'uburezi. Indi nyungu ni ukumenyekana kw'iki gihugu ku isi, ariko ikibabaje ni uko iyi nyungu akenshi ikumirwa n'inkuta ndende z'amahoteli abakerarugendo benshi batigera bambuka. Ikibazo mbona ni ukwangirika kw'umuco waho. Urugero rw'iki kibazo ni ukwimuka kw'Abamasayi benshi bava mu gice cy'ubutaka bwa Tanzaniya (cyane cyane mu gace ka Kilimanjaro) bajya mu duce dufite ubukerarugendo bwinshi (ku nyanja za Zanzibar). Aba Masayi bagurisha ibintu bito bito kandi babaye nk'ikintu cyo kurebwa n'abakerarugendo. Abamasayi bizera ko iyo umuntu abafotoye, amwiba ubugingo. Icyakora, mu duce tw'ubukerarugendo, ku mafaranga make cyane bemera gufotorwa.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]

TITLE: Ishusho y'umuntu
SLUG: portret-osobnosti
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/portret-osobnosti/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/portret-osobnosti.mdx
BUILT_HTML: portret-osobnosti/index.html
============================================================
Ishusho y'umuntu
 
 Ukuboza 2018 
 
 
 
Uyu mushinga wakozwe nk'akazi k'ishuri ku isomo ry'Ifoto mu Ishuri ry'Imyuga y'Ubucuruzi, Ubuhanzi n'Igishushanyo mbonera i Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Intego yari iyo guhuza mu buryo bw'igerageza ifoto y'umuntu, imiterere y'imitekerereze y'umuntu, ifoto yerekanywe, amashusho yimuka n'amajwi. Imiterere yanyuma y'amafoto n'amashusho yashingiye ku kizamini cy'imiterere ya Eysenck no ku gucamo ibice imiterere y'abantu hakurikijwe imiterere ine ya Galen.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/portret-osobnosti-obr-1.jpg | Intangiriro, ikizamini cya Eysenck]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/portret-osobnosti-obr-1.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/portret-osobnosti-obr-2.jpg]

TITLE: Ikirangamiterere cy'urubaho rw'ishashi
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Ikirangamiterere cy'urubaho rw'ishashi
 
 Ukuboza 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
Ku ifoto y'icyitegererezo, nshyizeho ikirangamiterere cy'urubaho rw'ishashi, aho buri kazu k'urubaho kangana na pixel imwe y'ifoto. Ubuso bwera bw'urubaho nyuma yo gushyiraho iki kirangamiterere ni bwo pixel z'umwimerere z'ifoto, naho ubuso bw'umukara bukaba ari ubuhinduye amabara n'urumuri. Ifoto mu bunini bwayo bwuzuye rero isa n'ubuso bw'ivu busanzwe, ariko iyo uyegereje (zoom) mu buryo bw'ikoranabuhanga, ifoto y'umwimerere iragaragara. Kugira ngo yerekanwe neza kuri ecran ya Full HD, ifoto igomba kugira uburebure bwa hafi 2000 px. Kwerekana nabi bishobora kubaho iyo ufunguye ifoto muri mushakisha (browser) ihindura imiterere ya pixel mu buryo bwa bicubic. Mushakisha yizewe ni nko muri Windows Photo Viewer.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
Nk'ikindi cyitegererezo cy'imikoreshereze y'iyi ntego, nakoze ifoto irimo itandukaniro ry'amabara ndetse n'iry'ibisobanuro.

============================================================
Ikoroshya ry'amakuru
 
 Kanama 2018 
 
 
 
Ni abantu bangahe bapakira amakuru tugerwaho n'inkuru tubona kuri interineti? Ese ukuri kwayo kwizewe? Isi tubona binyuze mu ndorerwamo z'amatelefoni yacu, mudasobwa na televiziyo itandukanye ite n'ukuri?
 
Buri makuru tubona ni ingenzi kuyagenzura mu masoko menshi kandi ntituyemere nk'ukuri gusa.
 
Iki gihangano cyakozwe binyuze mu gufata ifoto imwe hanyuma tugafata amafoto y'iyo foto nyirizina inshuro nyinshi kuri ecran ya mudasobwa.

Imyvugo ibiri
 
 mata 2018 
 
 
 
Videwo ya mbere igizwe n'imyvugo ibiri: imyivumbagatanyo y'ibigaragara n'urusaku rw'inkoni. Iyi myvugo yombi ifite igihe gito gitandukanye. Binyuze muri ubu buryo buto, videwo igera ku rwego rwo kutagira ituze mu gice runaka. Muri videwo ya kabiri, nongeyeho amashusho yerekeye insanganyamatsiko yo kurwanya intambara kuri uyu mushinga w'ibanze.

TITLE: Imibare y'impanuka yahiswemo n'abantu
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Imibare y'impanuka yahiswemo n'abantu
 
 Mata 2018 
 
 
 
Muri uyu mushinga, abantu batandukanye bahawe inyandiko y'ibibazo. Muri iyi nyandiko, abantu bageragejwe banditse imibare ijana y'impanuka mu ntera ya 1-100, imibare yabazaga mu mutwe. Nyuma yaho, nafashe iyi mibare nyishyira muri mudasobwa maze mpa buri nyandiko y'ibibazo igishushanyo kimwe. Umurongo utambitse wa x ugaragaza urukurikirane rw'imibare yihariye naho umurongo uhagaritse wa y ugaragaza agaciro k'imibare yahiswemo. Igishushanyo cy'ibara ry'ubururu cyakozwe na mudasobwa, ni ukuvuga, n'ikora ry'imibare y'impanuka. Mu kugereranya ibishushanyo byose, umuntu ashobora kubona ko buri muntu yageragejwe afite igishushanyo cyihariye gifite uburyo bwacyo bwo gutanga imibare. Nanone kandi, umuntu ashobora kubona ko mu bishushanyo byinshi hari ibihe bimwe byisubiramo bisa n'ibihuje (cyane cyane mu buryo bwo kuzamura no kumanura imibare buhoro buhoro).
 
IMIBARE YAHISWEMO N'IKORA RY'IMIBARE Y'IMPANUKA
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg | Igishushanyo cy'imibare y'impanuka yahiswemo na mudasobwa]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
IMIBARE YAHISWEMO N'ABANTU
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-2.jpg | Igishushanyo cy'imibare y'impanuka yahiswemo n'umuntu, Hana Antolová (imyaka 71)]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-3.jpg | Igishushanyo cy'imibare y'impanuka yahiswemo n'umuntu, Lukáš Plescher (imyaka 18)]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-3.jpg]

============================================================
Guhumeka nabi
 
 Werurwe 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/khCWjid0QeU?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Muri iyi nyikirizo y'amajwi y'iminota ine harimo inyandiko y'injangwe iri gupfa. Muri iyi nyandiko humvikana amajwi abiri y'ingenzi: guhumeka nabi n'ijwi rito ry'umutima utera.

TITLE: Mu ntekerezo y'igikonoshwa
SLUG: z-pohledu-musle
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/z-pohledu-musle/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/z-pohledu-musle.mdx
BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Mu ntekerezo y'igikonoshwa
 
 Werurwe 2018 
 
 
 
Aya mashusho yibanda ku kuntu igikonoshwa kiyumva n'ibyo kinyuramo. Imbonerahamwe yacyo ku isi ntiyoroheje ugereranyije n'iy'umuntu! Mu mashusho ya mbere, ushobora kubona amashusho y'umwimerere y'inyanja. Mu mashusho ya kabiri, ibumoso bw'ikirahure hagaragara ibara ry'impuzandengo ry'amashusho y'umwimerere. Iburyo bw'ikirahure hari igishushanyo cya RGB kigaragaza iryo bara.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/mxfAcE8KYaQ?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lZknZa20ilY?rel=0&showinfo=0]

Ubwoba
 
 Ugushyingo 2017 
 
 
 
Uruhererekane rw'amafoto rugaragaza ubwoba bwari mu gitabo cyanjye cy'ururimi rw'ikidage. Amafoto arondetse uko amapaji y'igitabo akurikirana. Bityo, buri foto ikaba irimo imiterere y'ibintu itunguranye, ishimishije kandi y'ubushakashatsi.

TITLE: Guhungabanya imiterere y'umurongo ihoraho
SLUG: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-10-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/index.html
============================================================
Guhungabanya imiterere y'umurongo ihoraho
 
 Ukwakira 2017 
 
 
 
Muri uyu mushinga, nagerageje gukora uruhererekane rw'ibihangano by'ubugeni bishingiye ku bitekerezo. Nagabanyije inyandiko mu bice bine. Mu gice cya mbere, nakoze imiterere y'ibanze y'umurongo ihoraho, mu gice cya kabiri nashingiye kuri iyo miterere maze ntangira kuyihungabanya buhoro buhoro bituma habaho kutagira ituze. Mu gice cya gatatu, nahungabanyije iyi miterere y'umurongo ihoraho y'imibare n'ishusho y'ibinyabuzima idahoraho kandi itandukanye. Naho mu gice cya nyuma cya kane, nakoze ayo mashusho y'ibinyabuzima mu buryo bw'amashusho yimuka.
 
Igice cya mbere:
 IMITERERE Y'IBANZE
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Ubugeni 1.0 - imiterere y'umurongo ihoraho]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
Igice cya kabiri:
 GUHUNGABANYA IMITERERE N'IMPINDUKA NTO

TITLE: Ibara y'impuzandengo
SLUG: barevny-prumer
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barevny-prumer/

SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barevny-prumer.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barevny-prumer/index.html
============================================================
Ibara y'impuzandengo
 
 Mata 2017 
 
 
 
Mu gice cy'ibumoso cy'ishusho harimo 1156 amafoto mu buryo bwa rasiteri ya 34x34 px. Buri pikseli y'ibara ihagarariye ibara ry'impuzandengo ry'ifoto imwe y'igihugu runaka. Ku gice cy'iburyo cy'ishusho hari ibara ry'impuzandengo ry'amapikseli yose yo mu gice cy'ibumoso, ni ukuvuga ibara ry'impuzandengo ry'amafoto yose y'icyo gihugu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-1.png | Repubulika ya Ceki]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-1.png] 
Repubulika ya Ceki

Kenya

Utubuto tw'isukari
 
 Werurwe 2017 
 
 
 
Iyi seriye y'amafoto yafashwe mu gihe cy'ibizamini by'impano ku Ishuri ryisumbuye ry'ubucuruzi, ubuhanzi n'igishushanyo mbonera i Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Ku gice cy'amafoto cy'ibizamini, twemerewe kuzana ibintu byacu bwite, ariko umubare n'ubunini byari bifite aho bigarukira. Nazanye agasanduku k'utubuto tw'isukari, nzigabanya ku isahani mu buryo bw'umurongo uhoraho hanyuma nshyiraho amazi. Utubuto twagiye dushonga buhoro buhoro, turagwa kandi twisuka mu buryo bw'impanuka.

TITLE: Isomero ry'ubuhanzi bwo mu muhanda
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: rw
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Isomero ry'ubuhanzi bwo mu muhanda
 
 Werurwe 2017 
 
 
 
Hirya no hino hose hari ibihangano byinshi bishimishije by'ubuhanzi bwo mu muhanda, uhereye ku dupapuro tw'amatangazo duto, inkuta zashushanyijwe kugeza ku bwoko butandukanye bw'ibintu 3D. Kubera ko abahanzi b'ibi bihangano bagomba kuguma mu bwihisho kubera ko binyuranyije n'amategeko, iyi si y'ubuhanzi bwo mu muhanda nta nyungu igamije kandi itandukanye n'isi y'ubucuruzi. Ibihangano byo mu muhanda dushobora kubona hafi ya hose mu mujyi akenshi biguma ari bishya. Ibihangano bishya byo mu muhanda bisimbura ibya kera, irangi rirashishwa hamwe n'inkuta, ibintu 3D bigirirwa nabi n'abantu, n'udupapuro tw'amatangazo tugenda twiyambura cyangwa tugacika amabara bitewe n'imiterere y'aho biri. Iyi ni inzira karemano, igice cy'igihe, ariko ndatekereza ko hari ibihangano bimwe na bimwe byo mu muhanda bikwiye kongererwa igihe cyo kubaho.
 
Ni yo mpamvu nafashe icyemezo cyo gufotora ibihangano bishimishije kurusha ibindi. Kuri iyi ntego, mu mwaka wa 2017 nashingiye urupapuro kuri Facebook rw'itwa Isomero ry'ubuhanzi bwo mu muhanda, aho nkomeza gushyiraho ibintu kugeza n'ubu. Kugira ngo ngera ku bantu benshi, nahise nshiraho urupapuro rufite iryo zina nyine kuri Instagram.
 
Kuri izi mbuga, ngerageza gushyiraho cyane cyane ibihangano bifite igitekerezo runaka, naho ubwiza bw'igihangano mbufata nk'ikintu cy'inyongera.
 
Isomero ry'ubuhanzi bwo mu muhanda (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Isomero ry'ubuhanzi bwo mu muhanda (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Ifoto y'urupapuro rwa Facebook]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Ifoto y'urupapuro rwa Instagram]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

Porogaramu ya Google
 
 Ugushyingo 2016 
 
 
 
Iyi videwo ngufi ni ubuhamya ku burenganzira budafite ishingiro bwa porogaramu ya Google yo muri telefoni (iyi porogaramu iba yashyizwemo mbere atari muri telefoni za Samsung gusa, ndetse iyi porogaramu ntishobora no gukurwamo). Incamake y'uburenganzira ishobora kuboneka muri telefoni ikoresha sisitemu y'imikorere ya Android muri Nastavení/Správce aplikací/STAŽENÉ/Aplikace Google.

============================================================
Ntibijyanye n'aho waturutse...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Nová scéna): Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. Ukuboza 2014 nafashe ifoto y'urukuta rwa kera rwanditseho muri Mombasa. Hanyuma nagerageje gusoma ibyari byanditseho. Igice kimwe cyasomekaga nagishyize muri moteri ishakisha ku rubuga rwa interineti maze mbona ifoto ikurikira.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 
 
 
 
Raporo ya Plzeň TV:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

============================================================
Ubuso
 
 4. 2. 2016 - 3. 4. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 Ibiro by'ubuhanzi by'umujyi wa Plzeň: MBERE, UBU, NYUMA 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/GALBEnzekBw?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Ubuzima bwacu bwo kuri interineti n'ubwo mu buzima busanzwe butandukaniye he? Kuki twajya hanze mu gihe byose dushobora kubibona kuri Google? Amakuru ava kuri interineti aragoreka kandi nta sano nini afitanye n'ukuri.
 Ni ishusho y'ibyo twifuza kubona.
 Izuba rirasa ku buso bw'amazi, rikagera ku rukuta kandi rikangara mu ndorerwamo... Ese koko ryatugezeho ritahindutse?
 
Incamake y'imurikabikorwa:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmsbQt0g9nc
 
Menya byinshi ku imurikabikorwa ku rubuga rw'Ibiro by'ubuhanzi by'umujyi wa Plzeň:
 http://galerie-plzen.cz/vystavy/vystavy-2016/predtim-ted-potom

TITLE: Kuva mu mujyi kugeza ku mahirwe
SLUG: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu

BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Kuva mu mujyi kugeza ku mahirwe
 
 Mutarama 2016 
 
 
 
Mu mwaka wa 2016 nahawe inshingano zo gukora igishushanyo cy'umujyi nk'igice cy'imyiteguro yanjye yo kwinjira mu ishuri ry'ubuhanzi rya ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14. Nashimishijwe no kumenya aho icyo gishushanyo cyashoboraga kugeza, bityo nkomeza gukora kuri uyu mushinga mu myaka yakurikiyeho kugeza ubu (umwaka wa 2019). Kuva ku gishushanyo cyoroshye cy'umujyi, nageze ku guhungabanya imbonerahamwe zihoraho, ibyo bikaba byaratumaga habaho imyitwarire itangaje mu rwego rw'ubuhanzi. Byongeye kandi, nagerageje gukoresha amahirwe muri uyu mushinga, ariko nta kintu na kimwe nigeze mbona nk'amahirwe nyayo adafite impamvu. Determinisme yambuzaga buri gihe, bityo nkoresha gusa amahirwe y'impimbano (impamvu ishobora kumenyekana, ariko ibisubizo bikaba bigoye kubihanura bityo bigasa n'aho ari amahirwe). Inzira zimwe zanjyanye mu nzira ifunze, bityo ngomba gushaka indi ntera n'ubundi buryo bwo gushushanya, urugero: gupanga ibyoherezwa by'ishusho, ingaruka za moiré, urukurikirane rwa Fibonacci, n'ibindi. Urutonde rw'amashusho ari hasi aha ni incamake y'iyi mirimo.

============================================================
Ibyatsi
 
 Gicurasi 2008 
 
 
 
Umurimo wo mu ishuri ry'incuke.
 
Icyo twasabwe kwari uguca indabyo ziri ku ndabyo mu mpapuro z'amabara no kuziteranya ngo dukore ishusho.
 
Aho gukora indabyo nakoze plevel.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg | plevel]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg]
